# Raw device logcat kept as evidence (2026-10-19, 14:08 run, status 4.82x)

Two captures of the same run - `femacs-launch.log` and `femacs-logcat-full.log` - moved out of
`/tmp` (R40).  They are the only record of that run's system side; the app's own lines are the
`android_run_debug_thread` ones.  Later, smaller captures live in
`docs/baseline/device-run-4.829.txt`; `tools/device-run.sh` regenerates this format.
